Result: 在Dense 1.5B、MoE 7.9B和MoE 124B长期预训练中，UFP4相比E2M1基线持续获得更低的BF16相对损失退化，并通过缩放律分析和消融实验验证。

Conclusion: 建议未来加速器应将E1M2/INT4风格的均匀4-bit网格作为与E2M1同等的一等训练原语来支持。

Abstract: FP4 training promises substantial reductions in memory and computation cost for LLM pretraining, yet current FP4 hardware paths and recipes, including NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin-class systems and AMD MI350-series GPUs, remain centered on E2M1 data elements. In this study, we identify a fundamental limitation of that choice: non-uniform formats such as E2M1 inherently suffer from Shrinkage Bias, a systematic negative rounding error caused by the geometric asymmetry of their representable bins. We show that this bias accumulates multiplicatively across layers and is amplified by the Random Hadamard Transform (RHT), providing a unified explanation for the training instability observed in existing E2M1-based FP4 recipes. In contrast, uniform grids (E1M2/INT4) bypass this grid-geometry error and better convert the improved bucket utilization from RHT into higher quantization quality. Based on this finding, we propose UFP4, a uniform 4-bit training recipe that applies RHT to all three training GEMMs while restricting stochastic rounding to dY alone. On Dense 1.5B, MoE 7.9B, and MoE 124B long-run pretraining, UFP4 consistently achieves lower BF16-relative loss degradation than strong E2M1-based baselines, supported by scaling-law analysis and ablation studies. Our results suggest that future accelerators should support E1M2/INT4-style uniform 4-bit grids as first-class training primitives alongside E2M1.

</details>


### [74] [Interpretable Sperm Morphology Classification via Attention-Guided Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20438)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Lars Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于注意力引导的深度学习框架（EfficientNet-B0 + CBAM），用于精子形态分类，在SMIDS和HuSHem数据集上分别达到90.2%和93.9%的准确率，并通过Grad-CAM++可视化提供可解释性，优于SimpleCNN和标准EfficientNet-B0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育是夫妇不孕的主要原因，常与异常精子形态相关。现有深度学习模型虽可实现自动分析，但缺乏可解释性，限制了其在临床中的应用。

Method: 将预训练的EfficientNet-B0与卷积块注意力模块（CBAM）结合，构建注意力引导的深度学习框架；使用Grad-CAM++生成热力图可视化，增强模型决策的可解释性。

Result: 在SMIDS数据集上准确率90.2%，宏F1分数0.913；在HuSHem数据集上准确率93.9%，宏F1分数0.948，均优于SimpleCNN和标准EfficientNet-B0。

Conclusion: 该准确且透明的框架可作为生育诊所中自动化精子分析的实用工具，具备临床推广潜力。

Abstract: Male infertility is a major cause of couple infertility, often linked to abnormal sperm morphology. While deep learning models offer automated analysis, most lack interpretability, limiting their clinical adoption. This study proposes an attention-guided deep learning framework for sperm morphology classification. We combine a pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to focus on key areas of the sperm head, improving both accuracy and interpretability. Evaluated on the SMIDS and HuSHem public datasets, our model achieves accuracies of 90.2% and 93.9% (macro F1 scores of 0.913 and 0.948), outperforming SimpleCNN and standard EfficientNet-B0. Furthermore, we use Grad-CAM++ visualizations to highlight features influencing the model's decisions. The results demonstrate that this accurate and transparent framework is a practical tool for automated sperm analysis in fertility clinics.

</details>


### [75] [Context-Aware Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling of IVF Laboratory Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20459)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Pia Saldeen,Lars Johansson,Thomas Ebner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用55个上下文感知的时间特征（如热稳定性、温湿度联合达标时长、峰值压力持续时间等）替代原始传感器平均值，结合分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型，将胚胎实验室环境数据用于IVF妊娠率预测。在两个不同地区的诊所数据上，该方法将预测误差降至1.27%（原始平均值为3-5%），并实现跨诊所的知识迁移（R²=0.86，35-39岁组误差降低64%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IVF妊娠率建模主要依赖患者层面变量，高分辨率的实验室环境数据未被充分利用，原始传感器平均值丢失了关键的时间动态信息。

Method: 1) 从原始传感器数据中工程化55个上下文感知的时间特征（如滚动热稳定性、温湿度联合达标时长、峰值压力持续时间、压力后恢复速度）；2) 使用分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型，通过部分池化（partial pooling）在两个不同地区的诊所间共享环境效应，同时保留各诊所的基线差异。

Result: 在亚洲诊所61周数据上，交叉验证预测误差降至1.27%（原始平均值为3-5%）；在另一北欧诊所的留出数据上，R²达到0.86，35-39岁年龄组误差较基线降低64%。

Conclusion: 结构化环境监测包含临床上有意义的、可迁移的信号，跨诊所的模型迁移是可行的，为IVF妊娠率预测提供了新维度。

Abstract: IVF pregnancy rates are routinely modeled using patient-level variables, while high-resolution laboratory environmental data remain underutilized. We show that this is a missed opportunity. Rather than relying on raw sensor averages, we engineer 55 context-aware temporal features, including rolling thermal stability, simultaneous temperature-humidity adherence, peak stress duration, and post-stress recovery speed, that capture the dynamics of incubator microenvironments. On 61 weeks of data from an Asian IVF clinic, these features reduce cross-validated prediction error to 1.27%, compared to 3-5% for raw averages. We then train a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across an Asian and a Northern European clinic via partial pooling, while preserving site-specific baselines. On held-out data from the Northern European clinic, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and a 64% error reduction for the 35-39 age group over a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically meaningful, transferable signal.

</details>


### [76] [What Do Safety-Aligned LLMs Learn From Mixed Compliance Demonstrations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20508)
*Sihui Dai,Mann Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合良性合规示范（无害请求+有帮助回应）与有害合规示范（有害请求+有帮助回应），探究不同示范组合如何驱动模型的有害合规行为。发现良性示范既可减少也可增加有害合规，具体取决于模型；偏好优化是防止良性示范增加有害合规的关键训练阶段；示范排序存在强近因效应；模型在拒绝与上下文学习交互方式上存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究证明上下文示范可以越狱语言模型，但模型如何理解不同类型的合规示范尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示示范组合（良性vs有害）如何影响模型的有害合规行为。

Method: 混合良性合规示范（无害请求+有帮助响应）与有害合规示范（有害请求+有帮助响应）的不同比例和排序，在四个模型上测试三种关于示范组合如何驱动有害合规的假设。

Result: 1) 良性示范与有害示范不可互换，其效果因模型而异；2) 偏好优化是防止良性示范增加有害合规的关键训练阶段；3) 示范排序表现出强近因效应；4) 模型在拒绝与上下文学习交互上存在差异——部分模型即使在拒绝时也采用示范格式，另一些模型则在拒绝时覆盖所有上下文信号。

Conclusion: 模型从合规示范中提取的内容取决于示范内容、排序和训练方法。这项工作超越了证明基于示范的越狱有效，转而刻画其工作机制。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that in-context demonstrations can jailbreak language models, but it remains unclear how models interpret different types of compliance demonstrations. We study this by mixing benign compliance demonstrations (non-harmful request, helpful response) with harmful compliance demonstrations (harmful request, helpful response) and testing three hypotheses about how demonstration composition drives harmful compliance. Across four models, we find that benign and harmful demonstrations are not interchangeable: benign demonstrations can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. We further show that preference optimization is the critical training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from increasing harmful compliance, that demonstration ordering exhibits strong recency bias, and that models differ in how refusal interacts with in-context learning: some adopt demonstrated formatting even when refusing, while others override all in-context signals upon refusal. Taken together, this work moves beyond showing that demonstration-based jailbreaking works to characterizing how it works: what models extract from compliance demonstrations depends on demonstration content, ordering, and training methodology.

</details>


### [77] [Multi-LCB: Extending LiveCodeBench to Multiple Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20517)
*Maria Ivanova,Pavel Zadorozhny,Rodion Levichev,Ivan Petrov,Adamenko Pavel,Ivan Lopatin,Alexey Kutalev,Dmitrii Babaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Multi-LCB基准，将原有的LiveCodeBench（仅限Python）扩展到12种编程语言，用于评估LLM的多语言代码生成能力，并发现了Python过拟合、语言特定污染和多语言性能差异等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiveCodeBench（LCB）虽然已成为广泛采用的代码生成评估基准，但其仅限于Python，无法评估LLM在实际软件工程中所需的多语言代码生成泛化能力。

Method: 将LCB数据集中的Python任务转化为等价的12种编程语言任务，同时保留LCB的污染控制机制和评估协议，并与原始LCB格式完全兼容以自动追踪未来更新。

Result: 对24个LLM（包括指令型和推理型）进行了评估，发现了Python过拟合、语言特定数据污染以及多语言性能存在显著差异等问题。

Conclusion: Multi-LCB是一个严格的多编程语言代码评估新基准，解决了LCB的主要局限性，揭示了当前LLM在跨语言代码生成方面的关键缺陷。

Abstract: LiveCodeBench (LCB) has recently become a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on code-generation tasks. By curating competitive programming problems, constantly adding fresh problems to the set, and filtering them by release dates, LCB provides contamination-aware evaluation and offers a holistic view of coding capability. However, LCB remains restricted to Python, leaving open the question of whether LLMs can generalize across the diverse programming languages required in real-world software engineering.
  We introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs across twelve programming languages, including Python. Multi-LCB transforms Python tasks from the LCB dataset into equivalent tasks in other languages while preserving LCB's contamination controls and evaluation protocol. Because it is fully compatible with the original LCB format, Multi-LCB will automatically track future LCB updates, enabling systematic assessment of cross-language code generation competence and requiring models to sustain performance well beyond Python.
  We evaluated 24 LLMs for instruction and reasoning on Multi-LCB, uncovering evidence of Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and substantial disparities in multilingual performance. Our results establish Multi-LCB as a rigorous new benchmark for multi-programming-language code evaluation, directly addressing LCB's primary limitation and exposing critical gaps in current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [78] [FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20518)
*Harshit Singh,Ayush Pratap Singh,Nityanand Mathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEdit是一种面向冻结流匹配TTS的终身适配框架，通过文本嵌入空间的token级扰动学习发音修正，并使用现代Hopfield网络作为可寻址情景记忆存储修正，无需权重更新即可实现OOV专有名词的发音纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配TTS系统在部署后静态不变，对于词库外的专有名词存在发音错误，除非重新训练模型，缺乏高效的终身适应机制。

Method: 在纠正反馈时，FlowEdit在文本嵌入空间优化token级扰动；将修正存储在作为内容可寻址情景记忆的现代Hopfield网络中；推理时通过带相似度门控的软注意力检索修正，实现模糊形态匹配。

Result: 在涵盖18个语系312个多语种专有名词的基准测试中，FlowEdit相较零样本基线将目标词音素错误率相对降低92.7%，同时保持通用语音质量不变，每次修正约需15秒（单GPU）。

Conclusion: FlowEdit通过隐空间条件编辑而非权重更新的方式，有效实现了冻结流匹配TTS的终身发音修正，兼顾了修正效率与语音质量保持。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [79] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSWIP提出了一种针对DeepProbLog程序的单世界反事实语义，通过神经物化、单世界干预程序（SWIP）和加权模型计数实现精确反事实推理，实验证明比DeepTwin方法速度快2.14倍，且能纠正神经校准退化导致的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号系统（如DeepProbLog）的标准推理是关联性的，缺乏因果语义支持反事实推理。现有方法（如DeepTwin）需要对内生变量进行重复编码，计算效率低。需要一种既能保持因果语义又能高效计算反事实的框架。

Method: 1) 通过神经物化将固定上下文中的神经谓词简化为普通ProbLog选择；2) 应用单世界干预程序（SWIP）语义；3) 在单个变换后的程序上通过加权模型计数（WMC）计算反事实。在有限基元和唯一支持模型假设下，该方法是精确的。

Result: 在MPI3D数据集上，通过12,000个查询验证了变换的正确性，推理速度比DeepTwin的孪生网络方法快2.14倍。在SUMO HOV实验中，展示了神经校准退化会偏置插件估计量，而正确范围化的随机策略AIPW估计量能消除大部分一阶偏差。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP提供了一种高效精确的反事实推理框架，避免了孪生网络的内生重复，同时通过商WMC形式识别活跃神经概率，解释了干预清洗、校准敏感性和稀有证据不稳定性等问题。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [80] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LedgerAgent，一种推理时方法，通过维护独立账本来记录任务状态并将其渲染到提示中，同时使用账本检查状态相关的策略约束，从而减少工具调用代理在客服领域中的状态管理失败和策略违规问题。实验表明，LedgerAgent在多个客服领域和多种模型上提升了pass^k指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准工具调用代理没有明确表示任务状态，而是将观察、工具返回和策略指令都放在提示中，导致代理每次决策时需从提示中重建状态。这种隐式状态管理引发两种常见失败：代理检索到正确事实但后续基于过时/缺失/错误信息做决策；语法上合法的工具调用可能违反依赖于当前任务状态的领域策略。

Method: LedgerAgent在推理时维护一个独立的账本来记录观察到的任务状态，并将这些状态渲染到提示中。在环境改变的工具调用执行前，使用账本检查状态相关的策略约束，从而阻止策略违规。

Result: 在四个客服领域以及多种开源和闭源模型上，LedgerAgent相比标准基于提示的工具调用方法，平均pass^k指标得到提升，在更严格的多轮一致性指标下提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 显式维护任务状态并用于策略检查，能有效提升工具调用代理在客服领域的策略合规性和状态管理鲁棒性。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [81] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将DAAM框架（原用于图像生成）迁移至语音扩散模型领域，提出跨注意力归因方法，用于分析风格描述文本如何影响语音合成中的声学输出。通过在CapSpeech-TTS上的大规模实验（3600个组合），揭示了风格token在时间、层间和ODE步骤中的注意力模式及其与声学特征的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格描述文本到语音合成（Style-captioned TTS）系统虽然能用自然语言控制语音风格，但单个词语如何影响声学输出的机制尚不明确。理解这一机制对于诊断模型失效模式、提升可控性至关重要。

Method: 提出跨注意力归因方法，将DAAM框架从图像领域首次迁移至语音扩散模型，应用于CapSpeech-TTS。通过提取25层、24个ODE步骤下每个token的注意力热图，分析了120个风格描述文本与30个文本转录组成的3600个生成组合。

Result: (1) 风格token的时间方差低于内容/功能token，证实其全局调节特性；(2) 风格注意力与基频(F0)和能量(energy)相关；(3) 风格条件作用在早期ODE步骤和深层网络中达到峰值；(4) 注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，恰与风格重要性峰值同时出现，表明网络在最关键风格阶段具有最大选择性。

Conclusion: 这是首次对自然语言如何影响语音扩散模型中跨注意力机制的系统性研究，为理解风格化TTS的内部工作机制提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [82] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究专家级校准（expert-level calibration）对混合专家模型（MoE）在分布偏移下行为的影响，发现硬路由模型中专家校准足以保证整体模型校准，但软路由模型不足；为此提出对抗性重新加权方法，改善准确率-校准权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明在个体预测器层面强制校准可提升集成模型及MoE的准确率与校准性能，但校准在何种条件下有助于MoE尚不明确，尤其缺乏对分布偏移场景下路由机制与专家级校准交互作用的理论理解。

Method: 1) 理论分析硬路由与软路由MoE在分布偏移下的校准行为；2) 提出对抗性重新加权方法，惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合的校准误差。

Result: 硬路由MoE中专家校准可保证整体模型在广泛分布偏移下的校准；软路由MoE中专家校准不足以保证整体校准；提出的对抗性重新加权方法在平均性能及困难子集上均改善了准确率-校准权衡。

Conclusion: 专家级校准在不同路由机制下对整体模型校准的影响有本质差异，对抗性重新加权方法可有效提升MoE在分布偏移下的鲁棒性和校准性能。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

### [83] [A land of monotone plenty, bis repetita: from classical to weak optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19516)
*Virginie Ehrlacher,Rodrigue Lelotte,Luca Nenna*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了c-循环单调性本质上是最优传输问题的零阶最优性条件，并将其推广到弱最优传输问题中对应一阶最优性条件，从而为这一单调性概念提供了全新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在揭示c-循环单调性与最优传输问题最优性条件之间的本质联系，并将这一视角推广到弱最优传输问题，从而统一和深化对单调性概念的理解。

Method: 通过将最优性等价于允许扰动径向锥上线性传输成本泛函的非负性，将c-循环单调性重新解释为零阶最优性条件；进而将该观点扩展到弱最优传输问题，证明其对应一阶最优性条件（弱传输成本泛函线性化的非负性）。

Result: 在经典和弱最优传输两种情形下，该单调性性质（在适当假设下）刻画了最优传输计划。经典情形下恢复了文献中已知结果但给出了重新审视的证明。

Conclusion: c-循环单调性在经典最优传输中是最优性的零阶条件，在弱最优传输中是其线性化的一阶条件，这一统一视角为最优传输理论提供了新的见解。

Abstract: The celebrated c-cyclical monotonicity property is shown to boil down to the zeroth-order optimality condition for the optimal transport problem. More precisely, we show that optimality is equivalent to the non-negativity of the linear transport cost functional on the radial cone of admissible perturbations. We then utilise this point of view to extend the c-cyclical monotonicity property to the weak optimal transport problem, for which it corresponds to the first-order optimality condition, namely to the non-negativity of the linearisation of the weak transport cost functional near the optimiser. Altogether, this sheds new light on this monotonicity concept. For both classical and weak optimal transport, we show that this property characterises (under suitable assumptions) optimal transport plans. In the classical case, we recover known results of the literature but with revisited proofs.

</details>


### [84] [On the Limits of Biased Derivative Information for Nonconvex Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19553)
*Anant Shyam,Brian Bullins*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在带偏导（非仅随机噪声）的非凸光滑优化中，寻找δ-驻点的问题。针对一阶方法给出了与上界匹配的紧下界，并建立了高阶方法的下界。提出了信赖域方法及高阶方差缩减方案，在高偏置场景下展示了高阶导数信息的优势，这是随机无偏场景无法实现的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非凸优化研究多聚焦于随机无偏梯度场景，但实际应用中（如强化学习、模拟优化等）梯度往往存在系统性偏置。作者旨在填补带偏导情形下非凸优化的理论空白，给出最优算法复杂度的下界，并探索高阶信息在偏置场景中能否带来超越无偏场景的收益。

Method: 1) 一阶情形：推导紧下界，证明找到O((ε+B²)^{1/2})-驻点的复杂度，匹配Ajalloeian和Stich (2020)的上界。2) 高阶情形：建立依赖偏置B_max的下界。3) 算法设计：提出信赖域方法在一定偏置范围内匹配下界；针对高偏置场景，设计高阶方差缩减方案以改进复杂度。

Result: 一阶情形下最优复杂度与偏置平方根相关；高阶情形下复杂度与最大偏置线性相关。信赖域方法在特定偏置范围内达到理论下界。高阶方差缩减方案在高偏置设置中优于传统方法，证明了高阶导数信息可带来改进，而在随机无偏场景中此类改进已知不可达。

Conclusion: 带偏导的非凸优化中，一阶方法的复杂度下界已被闭合；高阶信息在偏置场景下能提供额外收益，突破了随机无偏场景的理论局限性。

Abstract: We consider the problem of finding $δ$-stationary points for $δ> 0$, i.e., $x \in \mathbb{R}^d$ such that $||\nabla F(x)|| \le δ$, for smooth, non-convex objectives, where the derivative oracles are not only stochastic but also biased. In the first-order setting, we provide tight lower bounds for finding an $O((ε+ B^2)^{1/2})$-stationary point, for $ε> 0$ and where $B$ is a bound on the gradient bias, matching the upper bounds of Ajalloeian and Stich (2020). We then establish bias-dependent lower bounds for algorithms that use higher-order derivative information for finding $O(ε+ B_{\max})$-stationary points, where $B_{\max}$ is a bound on the maximum bias for all derivatives. To complement these lower bounds, we develop trust-region based methods that, for certain ranges of bias, provide guarantees that match the corresponding lower bounds. We further improve upon the oracle complexity in high bias settings through a higher-order variance reduction scheme, in particular demonstrating the benefits, in some cases, of using higher-order derivative information, whereas such improvements are known to be unattainable for stochastic unbiased settings.

</details>


### [85] [Mean-Field Control with a Common Hidden State under Decentralized Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19639)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Ali D. Kara*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究多决策者共享隐藏状态、通过相同信道获取分散观测的最优控制问题。在无限智能体极限下，问题简化为单一智能体控制问题，通过策略空间上的确定性测度值控制问题建模，并给出了动态规划递归。证明了随机化控制动作对最优性必要，但混合策略非必需；无限人口最优策略对有限人口近最优，收敛速率为$O(1/\sqrt{N})$，随记忆长度指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多决策者在共享隐藏状态和分散观测场景下的最优控制问题，并建立有限与无限人口之间的近似最优性及收敛速率。

Method: 将问题转化为极限下的单一智能体控制问题，利用经验分布将多智能体耦合解耦；在策略空间上建立确定性测度值控制问题，推导动态规划递归；通过概率方法分析随机化必要性和收敛速率。

Result: 随机化控制动作对最优性必要，但混合策略非必需；无限人口最优策略对有限人口近最优，收敛速率$O(1/\sqrt{N})$，随策略记忆长度指数增长。

Conclusion: 本文建立了分散观测多智能体控制的理论框架，揭示了随机化在最优控制中的作用，并给出了有限人口近似最优性的定量收敛保证。

Abstract: We study optimal control of a system with multiple decision makers who share a common hidden state and receive fully decentralized observations through identical channels. The dynamics of the hidden state and the cost incurred by the agents depend on the agents' actions only through their empirical distribution. In the limit problem with infinitely many agents, the problem reduces to a single agent control problem where the agent affects the hidden state dynamics via the conditional law of the actions given the past values of the hidden state process. We formulate this problem as a deterministic measure valued control problem over the space of policies and provide a dynamic programming recursion.
  We first show that for the limiting problem randomization over the control actions is necessary for optimality. However, randomization over the selection of policies (i.e., mixture policies) is not required. We then show that the optimal symmetric policies designed for the infinite population problem are near optimal for the finite population problem.
  In particular, we establish convergence rates that decay with number of agents as $\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}$, and grow exponentially with the memory length used in the policy.

</details>

Abstract: Aided by the AI tool GPT5.5 Pro, we provide a counterexample to a conjecture made by Ramachandra and Natarajan (2025) [Pairwise independent correlation gap, Operations Research Letters, 107255, 6040].

### [87] [Learning Neural Maximal Lyapunov Functions on $\mathsf{SO}(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19669)
*Adeel Akhtar,Matthieu Barreau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对数映射的神经Lyapunov框架，用于学习SO(n)群上动力系统的最大Lyapunov函数，通过Zubov型特征描述最大吸引域，并推导了对数映射导数的显式可计算公式，采用两阶段算法平衡计算效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Lie群上演化的动力系统，经典欧几里得空间中的Lyapunov方法无法直接适用，建立稳定性保证是一个基础性挑战。

Method: 提出基于对数映射的神经Lyapunov架构，具有可证明的逼近能力；通过Zubov型特征刻画最大吸引域；推导了对数映射导数的显式、数值可计算公式；设计两阶段训练算法平衡计算效率与精度。

Result: 在低维非线性系统上进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为Lie群上的动力系统稳定性分析提供了有效的神经Lyapunov方法。

Abstract: Establishing stability guarantees for dynamical systems on Lie groups is a fundamental challenge, as classical Lyapunov methods developed for Euclidean spaces do not directly transfer to curved geometries. In this paper, we propose a framework for learning maximal Lyapunov functions for systems evolving on the special orthogonal group $\mathsf{SO}(n)$. Theoretically, we introduce a neural Lyapunov architecture based on the logarithmic map with proven approximation capabilities, and we formulate the learning problem via a Zubov-type characterization of the maximal region of attraction. A key technical contribution is the derivation of explicit, numerically tractable formulas for the derivative of the logarithmic map, enabling training through a two-phase algorithm that balances computational efficiency and accuracy. Empirically, we validate the approach on a low-dimensional nonlinear system.

</details>


### [88] [Stochastic Representations of Stationary HJBI-Type Variational Inequalities with Bilateral Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19705)
*Sheng Huang,Qingmeng Wei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带双边约束的平稳HJBI型变分不等式的概率表示，提出了两种互补的随机表示：其一是通过扩展控制空间引入额外停止符号的无限时域二人零和随机微分博弈；其二是通过二人零和混合控制-停止随机微分博弈，其中每名玩家同时选择连续控制和停止决策，收益由带随机终端时间的BSDE定义。两种表示均证明值函数是对应HJBI变分不等式的唯一有界粘性解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为带有双边障碍的平稳HJBI型变分不等式建立概率表示，从而将偏微分方程问题转化为随机博弈问题，以便利用博弈论和随机分析工具进行研究。

Method: （1）通过扩大控制空间加入两个额外停止符号，将障碍项纳入运行收益，构建增强型无限时域二人零和随机微分博弈；（2）构建二人零和混合控制-停止随机微分博弈，引入依赖于对手控制过程的非预期停止策略以适配Elliott-Kalton策略框架，收益由随机终端时间BSDE定义；采用惩罚型无限时域随机微分博弈、动态规划论证及向后半群稳定性估计进行证明。

Result: 两种表示的下值函数和上值函数均是相应HJBI双边变分不等式的唯一有界粘性解。

Conclusion: 成功建立了带双边约束的平稳HJBI型变分不等式的两种概率表示，为这类变分不等式的求解和分析提供了新的随机博弈视角和工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study probabilistic representations for stationary HJBI-type variational inequalities with bilateral constraints. We provide two complementary stochastic representations.The first representation is obtained through an augmented infinite-horizon two-player zero-sum stochastic differential game (SDG). By enlarging the control spaces with two additional stopping symbols, the obstacle terms are incorporated into the running payoff. Using the framework of infinite-horizon stochastic recursive differential games, we show that the resulting lower and upper value functions are the unique bounded viscosity solutions of the corresponding HJBI variational inequalities. The second representation is given by a two-player zero-sum mixed control--stopping SDG. In this formulation, each player chooses both a continuous control and a stopping decision, and the payoff is defined by a BSDE with a random terminal time. To make the stopping component compatible with the Elliott--Kalton strategy framework, we introduce nonanticipative stopping strategies depending on the opponent's control process. The proof is based on penalized infinite-horizon SDGs coupled with their own value functions, together with dynamic programming arguments and stability estimates for backward semigroups. We prove that the value functions of the mixed control--stopping game coincide with the unique bounded viscosity solutions of the bilateral HJBI variational inequalities.

</details>


### [89] [Signature Methods for Optimal Market Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19772)
*Alberto Gennaro,Thibaut Mastrolia,Francesca Primavera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于特征的签名方法（Sig-REINFORCE算法），用于在均值-方差准则下求解最优做市问题，通过签名线性化技术将做市问题简化为对增广市场路径期望签名的伪线性优化，并在泊松过程和自激励霍克斯过程两种场景下与PPO基线进行对比测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统做市问题在高频交易环境下计算复杂度高，现有方法难以同时兼顾均值-方差准则下的最优性和计算效率。本文旨在开发一种能够利用路径签名线性化技术高效求解最优买卖报价的新方法。

Method: 采用签名线性化技术（Signature Linearization）将市场做市问题转化为对增广市场路径期望签名的伪线性优化问题，并设计了名为Sig-REINFORCE的签名算法来学习最优的买卖报价。

Result: 在两种市场订单到达场景（泊松过程和自激励霍克斯过程）下进行了测试，并以PPO算法作为基线进行了对比。

Conclusion: Sig-REINFORCE算法能够有效求解均值-方差准则下的最优做市问题，相比PPO基线方法具有竞争力，展示了签名方法在金融做市领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose a signature-based method to solve the optimal market-making problem under a mean-variance criterion. By exploiting signature linearization techniques, we reduce the market-making problem to a pseudo-linear optimization over the expected signature of an augmented market path, and we develop a signature algorithm named Sig-REINFORCE to learn the optimal bid and ask quotes. We test our method in two scenarios, in which market-order arrivals follow either a Poisson or a self-exciting Hawkes process, and we benchmark it against a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) baseline.

</details>


### [90] [Dynamic Core Allocation for Malleable Jobs with Unknown Speed-up Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19789)
*S. ~A. Bodas,J. ~L. Dorsman,M. Mandjes,L. Ravner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对多核计算系统中可塑性作业的动态资源分配问题，提出了一种迭代学习与控制框架，在未知速度函数参数下通过最大似然估计与MDP求解交替进行，以最小化长期平均作业数（即平均响应时间），并证明了估计量的强一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多核系统中可塑性作业的并行度可动态调整，但不同作业类的加速函数参数未知，现有方法无法在不确定性下实现最优资源分配。目标是在线学习未知参数并优化核分配策略，以最小化稳态平均响应时间。

Method: 提出迭代学习-控制框架：基于观测到的作业完成时间构造状态相关的最大似然估计器，证明固定策略下估计量的强一致性；将估计参数代入Berg等人MDP模型求解最优分配策略；设计两种结合估计与DP策略更新的学习算法。

Result: 证明了最大似然估计量在固定分配策略下的强一致性；通过数值实验展示了两种学习算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效在参数未知的多类可塑性作业场景下在线学习并优化核分配，降低平均响应时间；估计量的理论保证为算法收敛性提供了基础。

Abstract: We study dynamic resource allocation in a multicore computing system with a fixed number of processing cores and a stream of {\it malleable} jobs. Each job may adjust its level of parallelism during execution, allowing adaptive redistribution of resources across concurrently active jobs. Jobs belong to one of two observable classes, each characterized by a distinct speed-up function with unknown parameters. The objective is to learn a core-allocation policy that minimizes the long-run mean number of jobs in the system, equivalently the mean response time in steady state.
  \noindent To address this uncertainty, we develop an iterative learning-and-control framework. The system alternates between estimating the unknown speed-up parameters from observed job completions and solving the associated Markov decision process (MDP) to update the allocation policy. Within each job class, cores are shared equally among active jobs; the fraction of capacity assigned to each class is obtained from the MDP formulation of \cite{berg2017}, evaluated at the current parameter estimates. We construct a maximum likelihood estimator based on state-dependent inter-departure times and prove its strong consistency under a fixed allocation policy. We further propose two learning algorithms that combine this estimation step with dynamic programming-based policy updates, and illustrate their through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [91] [Semiglobal Input-Delay Tolerance Algorithm for Distributed Nonconvex Optimization of Networked Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19871)
*Jing-Zhe Xu,Zhi-Wei Liu,Ming-Feng Ge,Yan-Wu Wang,Dinxin He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对具有输入时滞和一致性约束的分布式优化问题，提出了一种新的半全局输入时滞容忍算法，在非线性网络系统中实现了时滞容忍的半全局收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法大多未考虑输入时滞与非线性动力学的耦合效应，而实际网络系统中输入时滞普遍存在且可能破坏系统稳定性与收敛性。

Method: 基于分层设计和输入-状态稳定性分析，提出SIDT算法；通过Polyak-Łojasiewicz条件放宽严格凸性要求，将算法扩展至非凸优化场景。

Result: 对于任意给定的紧致初始集，存在可容许的时滞上界，使得在一致性约束下所有节点状态收敛到最优解；数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: SIDT算法在理论上实现了非线性网络系统中输入时滞下的分布式优化，且通过松弛凸性条件扩展了应用范围，为实际系统的时滞容忍优化提供了理论保障。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of distributed optimization problems in networked nonlinear systems (NNSs) subject to input delays and consensus constraints. It introduces input-delay tolerant semiglobal convergence (IDTSC), meaning that for any prescribed compact initial set there exists an admissible delay bound under which the optimal solution is computed within consensus constraints and all node states converge to the solution. Building on a hierarchical design and input-to-state stability analysis, a new semiglobal input-delay tolerant (SIDT) algorithm is developed that practically achieves IDTSC for distributed optimization under the coupling between input delays and nonlinear dynamics. Further, by relaxing strict convexity requirements through the Polyak-Łojasiewicz condition, the SIDT algorithm broadens its applicability to nonconvex optimization. Finally, numerical experiments corroborate the theory on NNSs with input delays.

</details>


### [92] [A Regularized Nikaido-Isoda Function Approach to Multi-Leader-Follower Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20013)
*Atsushi Hori,Takayuki Okuno,Ellen H. Fukuda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多领导者-跟随者博弈（MLFG）重构方法，利用正则化的Nikaido-Isoda函数和惩罚参数将MLFG近似为单层可微纳什均衡问题，无需跟随者博弈的导数信息或凸性假设，并在全局子解析条件下分析了原MLFG与重构问题的均衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLFG求解方法（EPEC或代入响应函数）存在可扩展性差、需要高阶导数信息等问题，限制了适用范围。

Method: 利用正则化Nikaido-Isoda函数，引入惩罚参数，将MLFG近似为单层可微纳什均衡问题。

Result: 新方法既不需要跟随者博弈的导数信息，也不需要每个跟随者问题的凸性假设，可处理更广泛的MLFG类别；在全局子解析条件下建立了与原MLFG的均衡关系。

Conclusion: 该方法在可扩展性和适用范围上优于传统EPEC和代入法，为求解更一般化的层次非合作博弈提供了新途径。

Abstract: A multi-leader--follower game (MLFG) is a hierarchical noncooperative game in which leaders compete at the upper level while taking into account the followers' best responses at the lower level. A typical approach to solving the MLFG reformulates it as an equilibrium problem with equilibrium constraints (EPECs) by replacing the lower-level game with its KKT conditions. Another approach, when each follower's response is unique, is to reformulate the MLFG as a Nash equilibrium problem by substituting these response functions into each leader's problem. However, both reformulations may lack scalability since higher-order derivatives may be required when solving the resulting problems.
  In this paper, we propose a new reformulation of the MLFG by exploiting a regularized Nikaido--Isoda function and approximating the MLFG by a single-level differentiable Nash equilibrium problem with a penalty parameter. The proposed reformulation neither requires derivative information on the followers' game nor assumes convexity of each follower's problem; hence, it can handle a broader class of MLFGs. Under global subanalyticity, we analyze the mathematical relationship between equilibria of the original MLFG and the proposed reformulation.

</details>


### [93] [Optimization with inequality constraints by the embedded gradient vector field method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20059)
*Petre Birtea,Ioan Casu,Dan Comanescu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过引入二次松弛变量，将不等式约束优化问题提升至光滑子流形框架，利用黎曼几何与嵌入梯度向量场方法，从几何角度重新推导了KKT条件、二阶最优性条件及互补松弛条件，并在双冰激凌锥示例上验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束优化方法（如拉格朗日乘子法）在不等式约束处理上缺乏几何直观。作者希望借助黎曼几何语言，为不等式约束优化提供一种统一、几何化的分析框架，从而更自然地揭示最优性条件的几何本质。

Method: 1) 引入二次松弛变量将不等式约束转化为等式约束，从而将可行集提升为扩展空间中的光滑子流形；2) 分析该约束流形的分层结构，根据约束激活情况自然划分；3) 利用嵌入梯度向量场形式，推导出拉格朗日乘子函数的显式行列式公式；4) 在每个分层上计算限制Hessian矩阵以得到二阶条件；5) 识别拉格朗日乘子的符号条件作为互补松弛条件的几何对应。

Result: 得到了拉格朗日乘子函数的显式行列式公式，重新表述了KKT一阶必要条件；获得了基于限制Hessian的二阶最优性条件；建立了乘子符号条件与互补松弛条件的几何对应关系；在双冰激凌锥示例中成功应用该框架，揭示了问题几何结构决定局部极小值数量与性质。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为不等式约束优化提供了一种全新的视角，将经典KKT条件、二阶条件和互补松弛条件统一在黎曼几何语言下，并为理解约束优化问题的内在几何结构提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for constrained optimization problems with inequality constraints through the introduction of quadratic slack variables. This formulation makes it possible to employ the language of Riemannian geometry and to solve the problem via the embedded gradient vector field method. We lift the feasible set to a smooth submanifold of an extended ambient space. The stratified structure of the resulting constraint manifold is analyzed in detail, yielding a natural partition according to which constraints are active. Using the embedded gradient vector field formalism, we derive explicit, determinantal formulas for the Lagrange multiplier functions directly from the geometry of the constraint manifold, recovering and re-framing the classical Karush-Kuhn-Tucker first-order necessary conditions without invoking the classical Lagrange multiplier method. Second-order optimality conditions are obtained by computing the restricted Hessian on each stratum, and a complete sign condition on the Lagrange multipliers is identified as the geometric counterpart of the classical complementary slackness condition. The theory is illustrated on the double ice-cream cone example, where the geometry of the problem determines the nature and number of local minima.

</details>


### [94] [Optimal Coarse Correlated Equilibria in Mean Field Games: Linear Programming and No-Regret Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20062)
*Luciano Campi,Federico Cannerozzi,Ioannis Tzouanas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间平均场博弈，引入了最优粗相关均衡的概念，建立了线性规划刻画，证明了最优LP粗相关均衡的存在性，并设计了具有显式收敛率的无遗憾原-对偶学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平均场博弈中，传统粗相关均衡仅保证无外部遗憾，但缺乏一个统一框架来优化与参与者自身目标不同的特定性能准则（如社会最优或监管者目标）。

Method: 首先将问题形式化为在平均场粗相关均衡中优化性能准则；然后建立线性规划（LP）刻画，证明最优LP粗相关均衡的存在性，并建立LP与原始概率设定之间的联系；进而基于外部遗憾约束的等价拉格朗日形式，设计了一个无遗憾原-对偶学习算法。

Result: 证明了最优LP粗相关均衡的存在性；给出了学习算法的显式收敛率；并通过数值示例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为连续时间平均场博弈提供了一套从理论刻画到算法实现的最优粗相关均衡框架，兼具存在性保证与可学习的收敛率。

Abstract: We introduce optimal coarse correlated equilibria for continuous-time mean field games. A coarse correlated equilibrium is a randomized recommendation scheme from which no player can gain by ignoring the recommendation and switching to an alternative strategy. The problem is as follows: a moderator selects, among all mean-field coarse correlated equilibria, one that optimizes a prescribed performance criterion, which may differ from the representative player's objective. After formulating the problem, we develop a linear programming (LP) formulation, prove the existence of optimal LP coarse correlated equilibria, and relate the LP characterization to the original probabilistic setting. Building on this characterization, we design a no-regret primal-dual algorithm, based on an equivalent Lagrangian formulation of the external-regret constraint, for learning such equilibria. We provide explicit convergence rates for the learning algorithm, and numerical examples illustrate the method.

</details>


### [95] [Beyond Averaging in John Ellipsoid Approximation: High-Accuracy Algorithms in the Leverage-Score Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20082)
*Xiaoyu Li,Junwei Yu,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示了计算对称多面体John椭球的现代算法中，$\varepsilon^{-1}$复杂度来源于认证（certification）而非精度（accuracy）本身。通过将问题转化为D-optimal设计形式，作者将复杂度拆分为认证、识别和精度三个成本，指出精度成本仅需双对数依赖：$C(\mathbf{A})+O(d^2\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$次查询，而$\varepsilon^{-1}$完全来自认证环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有leverage-score算法族（从Cohen等人2019年到最新工作）都需要$\Theta(\varepsilon^{-1}\log(n/d))$次迭代达到$(1+\varepsilon)$-近似，但$\varepsilon^{-1}$这一项的本质来源不明确。作者希望分离现代算法中混淆的三个成本（认证、识别、精度），并找出$\varepsilon^{-1}$的真正来源。

Method: 将John椭球问题等价转化为D-optimal设计问题$\min_{\mathbf{p}\in\Delta_n}-\log\det(\sum_i p_i\mathbf{a}_i\mathbf{a}_i^\top)$，其中leverage-score预言机恰是一阶预言机，$(1+\varepsilon)$-John保证等价于Frank-Wolfe间隙$g(\mathbf{p})\le\varepsilon d$。通过此字典将三个成本分离，并分别分析：1) 认证成本：迭代平均值作为证书的间隙精确为$\Theta(1/T)$；2) 识别成本：达到最优面的代价；3) 精度成本：在最优面上用damped Newton仅需$O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$步。

Result: 1) 揭示了$\varepsilon^{-1}$是认证假象（certification artifact），来自迭代平均值的间隙$\Theta(1/T)$；2) 给出了热启动加速方法：$C(\mathbf{A})+O(\sqrt{\kappa}\log(1/\varepsilon))$次查询；3) 识别最优面后，damped Newton仅需$O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$步；4) 总查询次数$C(\mathbf{A})+O(d^2\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$，精度依赖为双对数。

Conclusion: 精度不是障碍（accuracy is not the obstruction），$\varepsilon^{-1}$这一项完全来自认证环节。开放问题是识别成本（达到最优面的无条件界）和下界。该工作首次将John椭球问题的精度依赖降为双对数级别。

Abstract: The John ellipsoid of a symmetric polytope $P=\{\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{R}^d:\|\mathbf{A}\mathbf{x}\|_\infty\le1\}$, $\mathbf{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, is computed by a long line of leverage-score algorithms, from Cohen, Cousins, Lee and Yang (COLT 2019) to its successors [WY24, CLS+25], all reaching a $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation in $Θ(\varepsilon^{-1}\log(n/d))$ iterations. We separate this complexity into three costs the modern line conflates (certification, identification, and accuracy) and locate the historical $\varepsilon^{-1}$ in the first alone. In the equivalent D-optimal-design form $\min_{\mathbf{p}\inΔ_n}-\log\det(\sum_i p_i\mathbf{a}_i\mathbf{a}_i^\top)$, the leverage-score oracle is exactly the first-order oracle and the $(1+\varepsilon)$-John guarantee the Frank-Wolfe gap $g(\mathbf{p})\le\varepsilon d$; through this dictionary the costs come apart. The $\varepsilon^{-1}$ is a certification artifact: the uniform average of the iterates, the certificate used throughout the line, has gap exactly $Θ(1/T)$, however cheap each iteration is made. Pointed instead at the last iterate the same oracle is fast: a warm-started accelerated method reaches the guarantee in $C(\mathbf{A})+O(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries after an $\varepsilon$-independent setup $C(\mathbf{A})$, and once the optimal face is identified the facial problem is an unconstrained self-concordant minimization whose Hessian the oracle recovers exactly, so damped Newton needs only $O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$ steps, for a total of $C(\mathbf{A})+O(d^2\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries. The accuracy dependence is thus doubly logarithmic after an $\varepsilon$-independent, condition-dependent setup; the open problem is the remaining identification cost (a condition-free bound on reaching the optimal face) and lower bounds. Accuracy is not the obstruction.

</details>


### [96] [Robust $Q$-learning for mean-field control under Wasserstein uncertainty in common noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20356)
*Mathieu Laurière,Ariel Neufeld,Kyunghyun Park*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对Wasserstein不确定性下离散时间均场控制的鲁棒Q学习算法，结合量化投影与Wasserstein对偶重构，证明了同步和异步学习方案的收敛性与有限时间迭代界，并通过系统风险和流行病模型验证了算法的鲁棒性与性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在公共噪声定律存在Wasserstein不确定性时，离散时间均场控制问题的鲁棒学习算法缺失问题。

Method: 将量化投影方案与公共噪声空间上的Wasserstein对偶重构相结合，设计了同步和异步两种Q学习算法。

Result: 证明了算法的收敛性并给出了有限时间迭代界；在系统风险和流行病模型上的数值实验验证了异步实现与理想化Bellman迭代的性能对比、公共噪声误设下的鲁棒性-性能权衡，以及异步Q学习算法的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒Q学习算法在Wasserstein不确定性下具有理论收敛保证和良好的实际性能，有效处理了公共噪声定律误设问题。

Abstract: In this article, we present a robust $Q$-learning algorithm for discrete-time mean-field control problems under Wasserstein uncertainty in the common noise law. The algorithm combines a quantization-and-projection scheme with a Wasserstein dual reformulation on the common-noise space. We establish its convergence together with finite-time iteration bounds for both synchronous and asynchronous learning schemes. Numerical experiments on systemic risk and epidemic models compare the asynchronous implementation with an idealized Bellman iteration, illustrate the robustness-performance tradeoff under common-noise misspecification, and report the observed convergence behavior of the asynchronous $Q$-learning algorithm.

</details>


### [97] [Optimizing Agricultural Drone Operations: From Launch and Recovery Siting to Tiered Routing Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20239)
*Ethan Kolby,Josh Noble,Max Z. Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无人机农业喷洒作业的运营框架与基准分析，通过设施选址启发式算法（p-中位数）和分层路径分解方法，将计算时间从97秒以上降至1.2秒以下，同时服务面积仅减少4%，实现了分钟级规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业无人机作业利润空间有限，需要高效的日常调度规划。现有优化工具随问题规模增大呈指数级增长，亟需实用的启发式方法支持实际运营。

Method: 采用两阶段方法：1）设施选址阶段，比较混合整数规划（MIP）基线模型与p-中位数启发式算法；2）路径规划阶段，采用分层问题分解方法，将目标区域划分为6至8个空间聚类，分别进行路由优化。

Result: p-中位数启发式将运行时间从97秒以上降至1.2秒以下，性能下降仅4%；分层分解将计算时间降低一个数量级，服务面积损失极小。整个框架可在普通硬件上实现分钟级规划。

Conclusion: 该框架在实际运营中具有可行性和高效性。未来工作将融入天气建模、设施选址与路径规划的集成优化，并在不同田间几何形状下进行验证。

Abstract: Drones are increasingly used in agriculture, where tight margins demand efficient planning. Current optimization tools suffer from exponential runtimes as problem sizes grow, necessitating practical heuristics for daily operations. This paper presents an operational framework and benchmarking analysis for drone spraying operations. We evaluate the trade-offs between facility siting methods and tiered routing parameters. For facility siting, comparing a Mixed-Integer Program (MIP) baseline against a $p$-Median heuristic shows that the heuristic reduces runtime by three orders of magnitude, from over 97 seconds to under 1.2 seconds, with only a 4\% reduction in serviced field area. For route planning, a tiered problem decomposition approach partitioning the target area into 6 to 8 spatial clusters reduces computation time by an order of magnitude with minimal degradation in serviced area. This framework achieves minute-scale planning on commodity hardware, demonstrating operational relevance. Future research will incorporate weather modeling, integrated optimization of facility location and routing, and validation across diverse field geometries.

</details>


### [98] [Diagonal Hessian Approximation Based on Conjugacy Condition for Noisy Derivative-Free Optimization Problems in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20304)
*Morteza Kimiaei,Saman Babaie--Kafaki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模含噪无导数优化问题，提出一种基于对角近似共轭条件更新的进化策略方法，在噪声水平高时比传统矩阵自适应方法更高效稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模含噪无导数优化问题中，矩阵自适应进化策略(MAES)在噪声水平高时性能下降，因为排序和选择会误判信息点，导致重组不可靠、缩放信息弱化，尤其在高维场景下效率显著降低。

Method: 用对角近似替代全仿射缩放矩阵，基于共轭型条件构建；利用连续归一化重组位移进行保守对角更新，不估计梯度、次梯度或插值模型，也不从含噪排序中学习密集协方差信息。

Result: 计算成本低于全矩阵自适应方案和有限内存仿射缩放变体；在含噪基准问题上的数值实验表明，该方法与MAES型基线相比具有竞争力，且在噪声水平高、基于排序的选择不可靠时往往更高效。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过保守对角更新机制有效限制了不可靠选择信息的影响，在保持进化框架无导数结构的同时，为含噪环境提供了稳定的缩放机制。

Abstract: We consider large-scale noisy derivative-free optimization (DFO) problems in which only function values are available and gradient or subgradient information cannot be reliably estimated. Matrix-adaptation evolution strategies (MAES) and their limited-memory variants are among the most robust DFO methods under noise; however, their performance may deteriorate when the noise level is large. In such regimes, sorting and selection may misidentify informative sampled points, making the recombination step less reliable and weakening the scaling information used by affine or matrix-adaptation mechanisms. This can substantially reduce the efficiency of MAES-type methods, especially in high-dimensional settings.
  To address this limitation, we propose a DFO method that replaces the full affine-scaling matrix with a diagonal approximation constructed from conjugacy-type conditions. The proposed mechanism does not attempt to estimate gradients, subgradients, or interpolation models, nor does it learn dense covariance information from noisy rankings. Instead, it uses consecutive normalized recombination displacements in a conservative diagonal update, thereby limiting the influence of unreliable selection information while preserving the derivative-free structure of the underlying evolutionary framework. As a result, the method is computationally cheaper than full matrix-adaptation schemes and limited-memory affine-scaling variants, while providing a stable scaling mechanism in noisy environments. Numerical experiments on noisy benchmark problems show that the proposed method is competitive with, and often more efficient than, MAES-type baselines, particularly when the noise level is large and ranking-based selection becomes unreliable.

</details>


### [99] [A Single-Loop Minorized Dual Decomposition Method for Nonsmooth Multi-Stage Stochastic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20383)
*Dan Luo,Hailin Sun,Lei Yang,Yang You*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有非光滑复合目标的多阶段随机规划问题，提出了一种单循环极小化对偶分解方法，该方法通过构造极小化问题及其受限Wolfe对偶，并执行一步对称Gauss-Seidel非精确ADMM来生成下一次迭代，所提框架保持阶段和场景分解结构且适合并行实现，建立了三阶段和一般多阶段情形的全局收敛性，数值实验验证了其计算可行性和良好的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机规划问题具有复杂的阶段和场景分解结构，现有方法在处理非光滑复合目标时效率有限，缺乏能够同时保持结构分解特性、支持并行计算并保证全局收敛的高效算法。

Method: 提出单循环极小化对偶分解方法：每次迭代构造一个极小化问题及其受限Wolfe对偶，然后在该对偶问题上执行一步对称Gauss-Seidel非精确交替方向乘子法（sGS-iADMM）来生成下一迭代点，从而保持阶段和场景的可分解结构。

Result: 建立了三阶段情形的全局收敛性，并推广到一般多阶段情形的全局收敛定理；数值实验表明所提框架具有计算可行性，且随阶段数和场景数的扩展性良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的单循环极小化对偶分解方法能有效求解具有非光滑复合目标的多阶段随机规划问题，具备结构保持、并行友好的特点，且具有严格的全局收敛保证和良好的数值扩展性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study multi-stage stochastic programming (MSP) problems with nonsmooth composite objectives. Tailored to their intrinsic stage-wise and scenario-wise structure, we develop a single-loop minorized dual decomposition method, in which each iteration constructs a minorized problem and its restricted Wolfe dual, and then performs \textit{one iteration} of the symmetric Gauss--Seidel based inexact alternating direction method of multipliers on the resulting dual problem to generate the next iterate. A key feature of the proposed optimization framework is that the resulting updates preserve the stage-wise and scenario-wise decomposable structure of the MSP problem and are suitable for parallel implementation. We establish global convergence of the generated iterates for the three-stage case and further establish the corresponding global convergence theorem for the general multi-stage setting. Numerical experiments illustrate the computational viability of the proposed framework and its favorable scaling behavior with respect to the stage-wise and scenario-wise structure.

</details>


### [100] [High-Probability Last-Iterate Guarantees for Two-Point Gaussian Zeroth-Order Stochastic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20446)
*Haishan Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对光滑强凸随机优化问题，建立了标准同样本两点高斯零阶随机梯度方法的高概率最后迭代收敛保证，证明以概率至少1-δ达到Õ(d/T)的收敛速率，且置信度依赖为对数而非多项式形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为同样本两点高斯零阶随机梯度方法提供直接的高概率最后迭代收敛分析，避免通过马尔可夫不等式从期望界转换或截断噪声的间接方法。

Method: 采用均匀加权扫描高斯角度与角度扩展乘积鞅边界相结合的技术，控制未展开随机递归中产生的带符号后缀乘积项。

Result: 在d≥16log(6T/δ)条件下，以至少1-δ的概率达到Õ(d/T)的收敛速率，置信度依赖为对数级别。

Conclusion: 首次给出了同样本高斯递归在条件次高斯随机梯度噪声下的直接高概率最后迭代结果，填补了零阶尺度上的理论空白。

Abstract: We establish a direct high-probability last-iterate guarantee for the standard same-sample two-point Gaussian zeroth-order stochastic-gradient method applied to smooth, strongly convex stochastic optimization. At each iteration, the method draws a fresh Gaussian direction, evaluates the objective at two symmetric perturbations using the same stochastic sample, and takes a norm-normalized stochastic-approximation step. Assuming unbiased stochastic gradients and a conditional exponential-moment bound on the squared norm of the stochastic-gradient noise, we prove that, whenever \(d\ge16\log(6T/δ)\), \[ f(\bx_T)-f(\bx^*) =
\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left(\frac{d}{T}\right) \] with probability at least \(1-δ\), up to fixed problem parameters and logarithmic factors. The confidence dependence is therefore logarithmic rather than polynomial in \(1/δ\). The analysis is direct: it neither invokes Markov's inequality to convert an expectation bound nor truncates the noise. We are not aware of a prior direct high-probability last-iterate result at this zeroth-order scale for the same-sample Gaussian recursion under conditional sub-Gaussian stochastic-gradient noise. The proof combines a uniform weighted scan for Gaussian angles with an angle-enlarged product-martingale boundary that controls the signed suffix-product term arising from the unrolled stochastic recursion.

</details>


### [101] [CLUSTER: Derivative-free optimization of smooth functions with parameter-change costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20498)
*Serena Landers,Sahil Pontula,Shiekh Zia Uddin,Sachin Vaidya,Marin Soljačić,Steven G. Johnson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出CLUSTER算法，针对每个参数（或参数簇）的调整有成本约束的局部无导数优化问题，基于Powell和Conn的二次插值优化方法进行改进，在多种测试问题（含光学实验）上性能提升约50%，大幅优于贝叶斯优化和Nelder-Mead算法，并给出了收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人控制的实验室实验等场景中，调整每个参数或参数簇都会产生独立的运动成本，现有无导数优化算法未充分考虑这种成本结构，导致效率低下。

Method: 基于Powell和Conn的二次插值优化算法，引入坐标级别的更新策略（CLUSTER），在信任域步骤评估中考虑参数调整成本，以更高效地选择更新方向与步长。

Result: 在多种测试问题（含光学实验）上，CLUSTER变体相比基线方法性能提升约50%，且大幅优于贝叶斯优化和Nelder-Mead算法。

Conclusion: CLUSTER算法能有效处理含参数调整成本的局部无导数优化问题，性能显著优于主流方法，且可适配Conn算法的收敛性证明以保证收敛性。

Abstract: We introduce the CLUSTER algorithm (\textbf{c}oordinate-\textbf{l}evel \textbf{u}pdate \textbf{s}trategy for \textbf{t}rust-region step \textbf{e}valuation \textbf{r}efinement) for local derivative-free optimization problems where there is a cost to changing each parameter (or clusters of parameters). For example, this type of cost model is appropriate for optimizing robot-controlled laboratory experiments, in which a robot may incur a separate motion for each parameter cluster to be adjusted. We build off of a class of quadratic-interpolation optimization algorithms by Powell and Conn that are known to perform well for twice-differentiable objectives (e.g. low-noise experiments), and show that the CLUSTER variants improve performance on a variety of test problems (including an optics laboratory experiment) by around 50$\%$, and greatly outperform common competing algorithms for laboratory optimization (Bayesian optimization and Nelder--Mead). We also adapt the convergence proof of the Conn algorithm to obtain a similar convergence guarantee for CLUSTER-Conn.

</details>


### [102] [On Second-Order Methods for Bilevel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20534)
*Jiawen Bi,Jiaxiang Li,Mingyi Hong,Shuzhong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对非凸-强凸双层优化的单循环三次正则化牛顿方法，实现了最优的$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$二阶稳定点收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层优化中寻找二阶稳定点的理论和实践尚未解决，即使下层问题是强凸的，上层超函数仍是非凸的。虽然三次正则化牛顿方法在单层优化中达到了最优SOSP率，但如何控制超梯度和超Hessian计算的精度以高效应用二阶方法于双层问题尚不明确。

Method: 首先构建了一个双循环CRN基线方法（达到最优外循环率但需重复求解下层问题）；随后提出单循环CRN算法，将一步下层梯度步与一步超梯度牛顿步结合，实现了总体确定性$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$最优总预言复杂度。

Result: 单循环算法达到了$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$的总预言复杂度（最优），且文中展示了一些直观的简单修改会导致收敛性失效。

Conclusion: 这是首个在无约束NCSC双层优化设置中，达到$\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$最优收敛率的确定性单循环方法，用于寻找超函数的$\varepsilon$-SOSP。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization is an indispensable modeling tool for modern machine learning and engineering design. However, the theory and practice for finding second order stationary points in the context of bilevel optimization still remain largely unsettled. Even for bilevel optimization with strongly convex lower-level problem, the hyperfunction it induces is in general nonconvex. Although the Cubic Regularized Newton methods (CRN) famously achieve the optimal $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ SOSP (second-order stationary point) rate in single-level optimization, it is unclear how to control the accuracy of the hypergradient and hyper-Hessian computations in the context of applying the second-order methods to bilevel problems in order for the overall process to be efficient. In this paper, we set out to answer this question. In particular, we first formulate a double loop CRN baseline that achieves the optimal outer rate but requires repeated lower level solves. Next, we propose a single loop cubic regularized Newton algorithm that combines one lower-level gradient step with one Newton step for the hypergradient, and prove an overall deterministic $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ total oracle complexity, which is optimal. In addition, we illustrate that some intuitively simple modifications of our method may fail to hold up the convergence result. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first deterministic single loop method for unconstrained NCSC (non-convex upper-level and strongly convex lower-level) bilevel optimization setting that achieves the $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1.5})$ optimal convergence rate for finding an $\varepsilon$-SOSP of the hyperfunction.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“计算可识别性”概念，区别于传统理论可识别性，通过有限计算搜索程序为经验估计量寻找满足误差容限的估计，以解决实际因果识别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论可识别性依赖于渐近性质、无限数据等理想化条件，无法处理有限样本、模糊图准则、混合数据等实际场景，因此需要一种计算驱动的可识别性框架。

Method: 定义“计算可识别性”框架：将识别问题转化为有限计算搜索过程，在指定参数先验分布和搜索程序条件下，寻找满足误差容限的经验估计量。

Result: 通过实验展示了该框架能回答细粒度的实际识别问题，包括：有限小样本识别、模糊图准则识别、观察与干预混合数据识别、反事实数据与估计量识别。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性为实际因果识别提供了更灵活、更具操作性的判断标准，弥补了理论可识别性在实践中的不足。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [104] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard)框架，通过上下文路由和不确定性门控温度机制，从分布偏移的时序基础模型中蒸馏知识到轻量级预测模型，在气象学、碳通量、土壤湿度和能源电网四个科学领域显著降低RMSE，适用于资源受限的边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序基础模型在物理科学中面临双重困境：零样本应用时存在严重的分布偏移，且计算成本过高无法部署于边缘计算传感器网络。核心挑战是如何从分布不匹配的基础模型中提取潜在结构知识来训练轻量级专业化预测器。

Method: 提出Guard框架，将多教师蒸馏重构为实例级决策过程，包含两个自适应机制：(1) 上下文路由器——基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关的教师模型，利用不同基础模型的互补性；(2) 不确定性门控温度机制——作为"断路器"，在教师置信度偏离领域现实时自动减弱蒸馏强度。

Result: 在气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源电网四个气候关键领域上，相比固定权重的多教师蒸馏基线显著降低RMSE。即使教师模型因分布偏移而零样本精度欠佳，仍能在28.5%的最难实例上优于全局最优的基础模型。

Conclusion: 证明了分布不匹配的领域教师模型仍可作为关键校正信号，实现了高精度科学预测，适用于资源受限的边缘部署场景。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [105] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏）方法，在边缘端使用4-bit量化小语言模型压缩用户提示词中的社交性冗余内容（如礼貌用语、寒暄等），在保持响应质量非劣效的前提下，平均每次调用节省99.9个输入token，预估每次调用净节能70-270μWh。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理的预填充阶段在云端产生巨大能耗，而用户提示词中包含大量社交性冗余内容（礼貌标记、道歉性前言、重复性内容等），这些内容对人类交流重要但对机器推理信息量低，形成了"社交-语义鸿沟"。

Method: SPSD是一个边缘端流水线，使用4-bit量化的Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) 作为小语言模型，在用户设备上压缩提示词，然后传输至云端Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct进行评估。关键设计包括：情感保留机制、规则门控的安全关键领域直通路由。

Result: 在248个提示词的语料上评估：平均每次蒸馏节省99.9个输入token，146次蒸馏调用全部产生正向节省；盲评中43%平局、28%蒸馏胜、29%原始胜，非劣效性检验表明在1分裕度内（15分制）不劣于原始路径；余弦相似度均值0.682，中位数0.712，54.1%超过0.70阈值；预估单次调用净节能70-270μWh。

Conclusion: SPSD证明设备端提示词蒸馏可以在保持响应质量（在实用的非劣效性边界内）的前提下，减少云端LLM的输入token成本并实现节能。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [106] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Med-DDPM扩散模型在三种NVIDIA架构上进行性能分析，发现训练主要由cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM核主导，存在内存访问、张量布局转换和Tensor Core利用率不足等效率问题。通过TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局优化，在A100上实现SM周期减少100倍、动态指令减少100倍、Tensor Core利用率提升至9.98倍、IPC提升7%，且不损失合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D MRI合成中部署面临巨大的GPU资源需求（每样本数百次U-Net评估及高度异构的内核行为），现有研究缺乏对医疗扩散模型在GPU上的细粒度性能瓶颈分析。

Method: 在Med-DDPM上跨三代NVIDIA架构进行内核级运行时分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、warp级活动和profiler优先级评分分析；提出TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局两种架构感知优化。

Result: 训练主要由cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM核主导；TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局优化实现SM周期减少100倍、动态指令减少100倍、Tensor Core利用率从1.45提升至9.98倍、IPC提升7%，且不损失合成质量。

Conclusion: 架构感知优化可大幅提升医疗扩散模型的GPU效率，在保持合成质量的同时显著降低计算开销。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [107] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型之间的形式化联系，证明ILL学习的概率规则可解释为基于商变量的约束因子图，其L2提升与最大熵原则密切相关，但信息格本身并非贝叶斯网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为信息格学习（ILL）提供严格的概率解释，阐明其与概率图模型（PGM）和最大熵模型的关系，从而为ILL的理论基础、推理方法、可识别性及符号-概率混合学习开辟新方向。

Method: 将ILL中的划分视为确定性商变量，规则视为该商变量的边缘分布；一般提升为满足这些边缘约束的所有联合分布族，特殊提升则基于L2均匀性（接近最大熵）选择最大无知重建；采用香农熵提升时，相同约束生成以学习到的抽象为因子的对数线性因子图。

Result: ILL学习的概率规则可解释为基于商变量的约束因子图；ILL的L2均匀性提升与最大熵原则密切相关；信息格本身编码的是抽象的细化和粗化关系，而非条件依赖关系，因此不是贝叶斯网络。

Conclusion: ILL应被视为可解释的基于商变量的约束因子图的结构学习方法，这澄清了ILL与图模型和最大熵模型的关系，并为推理、可识别性和混合符号-概率学习提供了新方向。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [108] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于双参数Weibull框架诊断Transformer权重分布，揭示了AdamW训练中权重尺度参数λ(t)经历增长、过冲、松弛三阶段的原因。通过推导AdamW更新的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力），发现对齐力主导上升阶段（贡献88-94%），而饱和期对齐力与衰减力趋于平衡。进一步提出了样条位移方法，从稀疏检查点以约92-94%的精度恢复对齐力。研究还观察到λ(t)峰值与训练数据一致性相关，暗示数据依赖的权重尺度增长成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdamW训练中Transformer权重分布尺度参数λ(t)为何呈现增长→过冲→松弛的动态行为，并探究其背后的力学机制。

Method: 从AdamW更新推导出权重平方范数的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力）；在自训练的Pythia-70M模型上使用真实优化器矩进行验证；提出样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 对齐力在上升阶段贡献88-94%的绝对力预算；对超权重移除鲁棒；饱和期对齐力与衰减力平衡；样条位移方法恢复对齐力精度约92-94%，约为朴素两基线方法的2倍；RMS到Weibull重构偏移约5-6%；λ(t)峰值随训练数据一致性变化。

Conclusion: 权重的尺度增长主要由对齐力驱动，饱和期由对齐-衰减力平衡解释；提出的样条方法有效解决了真实模型中优化器矩不可用的问题；数据一致性对权重尺度的影响值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [109] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多元零膨胀高斯分布的分布估计算法（ZIG-EDA），首次将EDA推广到稀疏黑盒优化场景，无需手工设计稀疏算子，联合优化稀疏模式与活跃参数值，在Lunar Lander基准上优于稠密EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布估计算法（EDA）在连续参数空间上表现优异，但尚未推广到稀疏参数空间（大多数系数为零）。现有的稀疏黑盒优化器不得不重新引入手工设计的稀疏算子、双层交替优化方案、零值阈值等，违背了EDA避免手工算子设计的初衷。

Method: 提出多元零膨胀高斯分布（ZIG）作为EDA的采样分布。该分布包含一个潜在高斯模型，通过独立的指示变量和数值变量分别表示稀疏模式、活跃参数间的相关性以及两者之间的交互，实现稀疏模式与活跃值的联合优化。设计了实用的基于摊销逆变换的估计器来估计潜在参数。

Result: 估计器能准确恢复潜在相关结构；在Lunar Lander基准测试中，ZIG-EDA收敛更快、最终回报更高，优于稠密高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA，且找到的控制器只有少数参数活跃。

Conclusion: ZIG-EDA成功将EDA推广到稀疏黑盒优化，消除了对手工设计稀疏算子的依赖，实现了稀疏模式与活跃值的联合优化。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [110] [Algebraic Dead Directions in LayerNorm Transformers: A Forward-Pass-Only Diagnostic at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19491)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar,P. J. Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LayerNorm变换器位于损失函数的奇异极小值附近，其中Fisher信息矩阵沿"死亡方向"退化。研究者识别出LayerNorm仿射参数的反尺度方向是后归一化中心化激活协方差矩阵的精确代数核，无需前向/反向传播或特征分解即可直接读取。该方法在14个预训练变换器上验证，随机初始化时预测方向与实测底部奇异方向精确匹配至小数点后四位，训练后该方向的协方差特征值加深约1000倍。该发现可用于从参数直接判断变换器的归一化类型，并揭示残差流最小奇异值的块间保持特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练变换器损失函数中存在奇异极小值，Fisher信息矩阵沿某些参数方向退化（死亡方向），但现有方法需要前向传播和特征分解才能定位这些方向，无法仅从网络参数直接计算。本文旨在寻找一种仅依赖LayerNorm参数即可计算死亡方向的方法。

Method: 理论推导证明LayerNorm仿射参数的反尺度方向（γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖）是后归一化中心化激活协方差矩阵的精确代数核。通过分析LayerNorm的均值减法投影算子，建立该方向与死亡方向的理论联系。在14个预训练变换器（9个LayerNorm，5个RMSNorm，参数规模160M-35B）上进行实验验证，比较随机初始化和训练后检查点的奇异结构。

Result: 随机初始化时，预测方向与实测底部奇异方向在9/9个LayerNorm模型上精确匹配至小数点后四位；在5/5个RMSNorm模型上正确缺失该方向。训练后该方向的协方差特征值加深约1000倍，并出现更多死亡方向。13/14个变换器的残差流最小奇异值呈块间保持特性，唯一的例外（Gemma4-31B）可由该方法定位其真正的死亡方向。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了目前最简洁的死亡方向读取方式（仅需LayerNorm尺度参数，无需任何计算开销），可用于从参数直接判断变换器的归一化类型，并揭示训练过程中奇异结构的变化规律。该方法仅适用于LayerNorm（因依赖均值减法投影算子），不适用于RMSNorm。

Abstract: Pretrained transformers sit near singular minima of the loss, where the Fisher information metric degenerates along dead directions: directions in parameter space along which the directional Fisher vanishes. Locating such a direction normally needs a forward pass and an eigendecomposition of activations, or a sampling-based complexity estimate; none returns a direction computable from the network's parameters alone. We give one, for LayerNorm transformers. The inverse-scale direction $γ^{-1}/\|γ^{-1}\|$ of the LayerNorm affine is an exact algebraic kernel of the post-final-norm centred activation covariance, for any input distribution, and induces a corresponding dead direction in parameter space. It is read from the LN scale parameter alone, with no forward or backward pass and no eigensolve: the cheapest dead-direction read, specific to LayerNorm. We test it on $14$ pretrained transformers ($9$ LayerNorm, $5$ RMSNorm; $160$M-$35$B; language and vision objectives). At random initialisation the predicted direction matches the measured bottom singular direction (one forward pass, direct SVD) to four decimal places on $9/9$ LayerNorm models, and is correctly absent on $5/5$ RMSNorm models, which lack the mean-subtraction projector that creates it. On the trained checkpoint the covariance eigenvalue along this direction deepens by ${\sim}10^3\times$ and further dead directions open; the random-init-to-trained gap is a one-forward-pass, per-checkpoint readout of singular structure along the predicted coordinate. Two consequences follow in closed form: the residual stream's smallest singular value is preserved block-to-block on $13/14$ transformers measured on their own input distribution, the one exception (Gemma$4$-$31$B) a genuine dead direction the same read pinpoints; and the kernel direction's presence classifies a transformer's normalisation from the parameters alone.

</details>


### [111] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPICE方法，在自我对弈强化学习中加入少量人类数据（30分钟）作为正则化目标，仅需15小时即可在单张消费级GPU上训练出能与人类协作的驾驶策略，所需人类数据比模仿学习少2500倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自我对弈强化学习训练的驾驶策略会学习到与人类不兼容的"外星"驾驶习惯，而现有通过奖励工程和域随机化缓解行为偏差的方法脆弱且劳动密集。

Method: 在最小安全目标达成奖励的基础上，将人类演示数据作为正则化目标叠加到自我对弈强化学习中，仅使用30分钟人类数据。

Result: 与未见过的真人轨迹协作良好，训练仅需15小时（单张消费级GPU），人类数据需求量比同类模仿学习方法少2500倍。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据（30分钟）即可有效引导自我对弈强化学习策略与人类行为对齐，实现高效、低成本的协作驾驶策略训练。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [112] [On the Oracle Complexity of Interpolation-Based Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19878)
*Dongmin Lee,William Lu,Anuran Makur*

Main category: cs.LG
